FILE: .\backend\app\application\announcements\announcement_operations_service.py
####################################################################
"""Application service for controlled Announcement Operations lifecycle."""
from __future__ import annotations

from uuid import UUID, uuid4

from app.domain.announcements.announcement import Announcement
from app.domain.announcements.publication_event import PublicationEvent, PublicationEventType
from .announcement_repository import AnnouncementRepository
from .publication_event_repository import PublicationEventRepository


class AnnouncementOperationsService:
    """Coordinates lifecycle changes and immutable audit events.

    This foundation intentionally does not deliver to Telegram. Delivery is a
    later adapter behind an explicit port so the domain remains provider-independent.
    """

    def __init__(self, *, repository: AnnouncementRepository, event_repository: PublicationEventRepository) -> None:
        self._repository = repository
        self._events = event_repository

    async def create(self, *, title: str, body: str, actor: str, correlation_id: str | None = None) -> Announcement:
        if not title.strip() or not body.strip() or not actor.strip():
            raise ValueError("title, body and actor are required.")
        announcement = Announcement(title=title.strip(), body=body.strip(), created_by=actor.strip())
        await self._repository.save(announcement)
        await self._audit(announcement, PublicationEventType.CREATED, actor, correlation_id)
        return announcement

    async def mark_ready_for_review(self, announcement_id: UUID, *, actor: str, correlation_id: str | None = None) -> Announcement:
        announcement = await self._require(announcement_id)
        announcement.mark_ready_for_review()
        await self._repository.save(announcement)
        await self._audit(announcement, PublicationEventType.READY_FOR_REVIEW, actor, correlation_id)
        return announcement

    async def approve(self, announcement_id: UUID, *, actor: str, correlation_id: str | None = None) -> Announcement:
        announcement = await self._require(announcement_id)
        announcement.approve(actor)
        await self._repository.save(announcement)
        await self._audit(announcement, PublicationEventType.APPROVED, actor, correlation_id)
        return announcement

    async def request_publication(self, announcement_id: UUID, *, actor: str, correlation_id: str | None = None) -> Announcement:
        announcement = await self._require(announcement_id)
        announcement.request_publication()
        await self._repository.save(announcement)
        await self._audit(announcement, PublicationEventType.PUBLISH_REQUESTED, actor, correlation_id)
        return announcement

    async def mark_published(self, announcement_id: UUID, *, actor: str, correlation_id: str | None = None, provider_message_id: str | None = None) -> Announcement:
        announcement = await self._require(announcement_id)
        announcement.mark_published()
        await self._repository.save(announcement)
        await self._audit(announcement, PublicationEventType.PUBLISHED, actor, correlation_id, provider_message_id=provider_message_id)
        return announcement

    async def mark_failed(self, announcement_id: UUID, *, actor: str, error_code: str, correlation_id: str | None = None) -> Announcement:
        announcement = await self._require(announcement_id)
        announcement.mark_failed()
        await self._repository.save(announcement)
        await self._audit(announcement, PublicationEventType.FAILED, actor, correlation_id, error_code=error_code)
        return announcement

    async def get(self, announcement_id: UUID) -> Announcement:
        return await self._require(announcement_id)

    async def list(self) -> tuple[Announcement, ...]:
        return await self._repository.list()

    async def audit_log(self, announcement_id: UUID) -> tuple[PublicationEvent, ...]:
        await self._require(announcement_id)
        return await self._events.list_for_publication(announcement_id)

    async def _require(self, announcement_id: UUID) -> Announcement:
        announcement = await self._repository.get(announcement_id)
        if announcement is None:
            raise LookupError(f"Announcement {announcement_id} was not found.")
        return announcement

    async def _audit(self, announcement: Announcement, event_type: PublicationEventType, actor: str, correlation_id: str | None, *, error_code: str | None = None, provider_message_id: str | None = None) -> None:
        if not actor.strip():
            raise ValueError("actor cannot be empty.")
        await self._events.append(PublicationEvent(
            publication_id=announcement.id,
            event_type=event_type,
            actor=actor,
            correlation_id=correlation_id or str(uuid4()),
            error_code=error_code,
            provider_message_id=provider_message_id,
        ))

####################################################################

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Mapping

from app.application.announcements.publication_service import (
    AnnouncementPublicationService,
)
from app.application.announcements.template_editor_models import (
    TemplateEditorOption,
    TemplateEditorPreview,
    build_editor_fields,
)
from app.application.announcements.template_registry import (
    AnnouncementTemplateRegistry,
)


class AnnouncementTemplateEditorService:
    """Application service for the template-driven publication editor.

    This service only prepares editor metadata, validates/preview-renders
    template values, and creates drafts. It has no channel/provider dependency
    and never performs external publication.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        *,
        templates: AnnouncementTemplateRegistry,
        publications: AnnouncementPublicationService,
    ) -> None:
        if templates is None or publications is None:
            raise ValueError("templates and publications are required.")
        self._templates = templates
        self._publications = publications

    def list_approved_templates(self) -> tuple[TemplateEditorOption, ...]:
        template_models = self._templates.list_templates()

        return tuple(
            TemplateEditorOption(
                template_id=template.template_id,
                name=template.name,
                category=(
                    template.category
                    if template.category
                    else template.publication_type.value
                ),
                description=template.description
                or "Approved announcement pattern.",
                version=template.active_version,
                publication_type=template.publication_type.value,
                fields=build_editor_fields(
                    template.get_version().required_fields,
                    template.get_version().optional_fields,
                ),
            )
            for template in template_models
        )

    def preview(
        self,
        *,
        template_id: str,
        values: Mapping[str, str],
        version: int | None = None,
    ) -> TemplateEditorPreview:
        result = self._publications.preview(
            template_id=template_id,
            values=values,
            version=version,
        )
        return TemplateEditorPreview(
            template_id=result.template_id,
            template_version=result.template_version,
            publication_type=result.publication_type,
            content=result.content,
        )

    def create_draft(
        self,
        *,
        publication_id: str,
        template_id: str,
        title: str,
        values: Mapping[str, str],
        version: int | None = None,
    ):
        return self._publications.create(
            publication_id=publication_id,
            template_id=template_id,
            title=title,
            values=values,
            version=version,
        )

####################################################################
FILE: .\backend\app\application\announcement_operations\publication_execution_service.py

"""WS-22.3.1 publication execution application contract."""

from __future__ import annotations

from app.application.announcement_operations.publication_execution_ports import (
    PublicationAuditSink,
    PublicationExecutionRepository,
    PublicationIdempotencyStore,
    PublicationPublisher,
)
from app.domain.announcement_publication.execution import (
    PublishAnnouncementCommand,
    PublicationAuditEvent,
    PublicationExecutionErrorCode,
    PublicationExecutionResult,
)


class PublicationExecutionService:
    """Execute an approved publication through an injected publishing port.

    This service contains orchestration only. It does not know Telegram,
    HTTP, frontend concerns, credentials, or provider-specific APIs.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        *,
        repository: PublicationExecutionRepository,
        publisher: PublicationPublisher,
        idempotency_store: PublicationIdempotencyStore,
        audit_sink: PublicationAuditSink,
    ) -> None:
        if any(
            dependency is None
            for dependency in (
                repository,
                publisher,
                idempotency_store,
                audit_sink,
            )
        ):
            raise ValueError("All publication execution dependencies are required.")

        self._repository = repository
        self._publisher = publisher
        self._idempotency_store = idempotency_store
        self._audit_sink = audit_sink

    async def execute(
        self,
        command: PublishAnnouncementCommand,
    ) -> PublicationExecutionResult:
        """Execute one explicit publication request."""

        existing = await self._idempotency_store.get_result(
            command.idempotency_key
        )
        if existing is not None:
            return existing

        publication = await self._repository.get_for_execution(
            command.publication_id
        )

        if publication is None:
            result = PublicationExecutionResult.failed(
                publication_id=command.publication_id,
                idempotency_key=command.idempotency_key,
                error_code=PublicationExecutionErrorCode.INVALID_STATE,
                error_message="Publication is not available for execution.",
            )
            await self._record_result(command, result)
            return result

        if publication.status.lower() not in {"approved", "ready_to_publish"}:
            result = PublicationExecutionResult.failed(
                publication_id=command.publication_id,
                idempotency_key=command.idempotency_key,
                error_code=PublicationExecutionErrorCode.INVALID_STATE,
                error_message="Publication is not in an executable state.",
            )
            await self._record_result(command, result)
            return result

        try:
            provider_reference = await self._publisher.publish(
                publication=publication,
                idempotency_key=command.idempotency_key,
            )
        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
            result = PublicationExecutionResult.failed(
                publication_id=command.publication_id,
                idempotency_key=command.idempotency_key,
                error_code=PublicationExecutionErrorCode.PROVIDER_ERROR,
                error_message=str(exc) or "Publication provider failed.",
            )
            await self._record_result(command, result)
            return result

        result = PublicationExecutionResult.succeeded(
            publication_id=command.publication_id,
            idempotency_key=command.idempotency_key,
            provider_reference=provider_reference,
        )
        await self._record_result(command, result)
        return result

    async def _record_result(
        self,
        command: PublishAnnouncementCommand,
        result: PublicationExecutionResult,
    ) -> None:
        await self._idempotency_store.store_result(result)
        await self._repository.record_execution(result)

        await self._audit_sink.record(
            PublicationAuditEvent(
                event_name="announcement_publication_execution",
                publication_id=result.publication_id,
                requested_by=command.requested_by,
                status=result.status,
                occurred_at=result.executed_at,
                idempotency_key=result.idempotency_key,
                provider_reference=result.provider_reference,
                error_code=result.error_code,
                safe_metadata={"execution_status": result.status.value},
            )
        )


__all__ = ["PublicationExecutionService"]

"""WS-22.3.1 provider/channel-independent ports."""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Protocol

from app.domain.announcement_publication.execution import (
    PublishAnnouncementCommand,
    PublicationExecutionResult,
)


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class PublicationForExecution:
    """Minimal execution projection required by the use case.

    The adapter/application layer can map the existing WS-22.1
    publication model into this projection without coupling the execution
    engine to a specific channel provider.
    """

    publication_id: str
    status: str
    destination: str
    rendered_content: str


class PublicationExecutionRepository(Protocol):
    """Port for loading and recording publication execution state."""

    async def get_for_execution(
        self,
        publication_id: str,
    ) -> PublicationForExecution | None:
        ...

    async def record_execution(
        self,
        result: PublicationExecutionResult,
    ) -> None:
        ...


class PublicationPublisher(Protocol):
    """Provider/channel-neutral publishing port."""

    async def publish(
        self,
        *,
        publication: PublicationForExecution,
        idempotency_key: str,
    ) -> str:
        """Publish and return an opaque provider reference.

        Implementations must not expose credentials through the result.
        """
        ...


class PublicationAuditSink(Protocol):
    """Port for durable/observable publication audit events."""

    async def record(self, event: object) -> None:
        ...


class PublicationIdempotencyStore(Protocol):
    """Port preventing duplicate external publication attempts."""

    async def get_result(
        self,
        idempotency_key: str,
    ) -> PublicationExecutionResult | None:
        ...

    async def store_result(
        self,
        result: PublicationExecutionResult,
    ) -> None:
        ...


__all__ = [
    "PublicationAuditSink",
    "PublicationExecutionRepository",
    "PublicationExecutionResult",
    "PublicationForExecution",
    "PublicationIdempotencyStore",
    "PublicationPublisher",
]

####################################################################
FILE: .\backend\app\application\announcement\announcement_publisher.py
####################################################################
from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from typing import Protocol


class AnnouncementPublisher(Protocol):
    async def publish(
        self,
        *,
        channel_id: str,
        text: str,
    ) -> str:
        """Publish an announcement and return its external message ID."""


class PublicationDisabledError(RuntimeError):
    """Raised when controlled announcement publishing is disabled."""


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class PublicationRecord:
    channel_id: str
    message_id: str
    announcement_type: str
    published_at: datetime


class ControlledAnnouncementPublisher:
    """Application boundary enforcing the explicit Beta 1 publication switch."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        *,
        publisher: AnnouncementPublisher,
        publishing_enabled: bool,
    ) -> None:
        if publisher is None:
            raise ValueError("publisher cannot be None.")

        self._publisher = publisher
        self._publishing_enabled = publishing_enabled

    async def publish(
        self,
        *,
        channel_id: str,
        text: str,
        announcement_type: str,
    ) -> PublicationRecord:
        if not self._publishing_enabled:
            raise PublicationDisabledError(
                "Telegram announcement publishing is disabled."
            )

        message_id = await self._publisher.publish(
            channel_id=channel_id,
            text=text,
        )

        return PublicationRecord(
            channel_id=channel_id,
            message_id=message_id,
            announcement_type=announcement_type,
            published_at=datetime.now(timezone.utc),
        )

def _required_environment(name: str) -> str:
    value = os.getenv(name, "").strip()

    if not value:
        raise RuntimeError(
            f"{name} is required for authentication email composition."
        )

    return value


def _create_announcement_components():
    telegram_client = TelegramAnnouncementClient()

    announcement_publisher = TelegramAnnouncementPublisher(
        telegram_client=telegram_client,
    )

    return (
        telegram_client,
        announcement_publisher,
    )


@asynccontextmanager
async def lifespan(
    application: FastAPI,
):
    scheduler_runtime = application.state.scheduler_runtime

    try:
        await scheduler_runtime.start()
    except Exception as exc:
        print(
            "Cybersecurity scheduler startup failed:",
            exc,
        )

    yield

    try:
        await scheduler_runtime.stop()
    except Exception as exc:
        print(
            "Cybersecurity scheduler shutdown failed:",
            exc,
        )
    finally:
        telegram_client = getattr(
            application.state,
            "announcement_telegram_client",
            None,
        )

        if telegram_client is not None:
            try:
                await telegram_client.close()
            except Exception as exc:
                print(
                    "Telegram announcement client shutdown failed:",
                    exc,
                )

        auth_engine = getattr(
            application.state,
            "auth_engine",
            None,
        )

        if auth_engine is not None:
            try:
                await auth_engine.dispose()
            except Exception as exc:
                print(
                    "PostgreSQL authentication engine shutdown failed:",
                    exc,
                )

        provider_usage_engine = getattr(
            application.state,
            "provider_usage_engine",
            None,
        )

        if provider_usage_engine is not None:
            try:
                provider_usage_engine.dispose()
            except Exception as exc:
                print(
                    "PostgreSQL provider-usage engine shutdown failed:",
                    exc,
                )


def create_application() -> FastAPI:
    academy_config = (
        AcademyActivationConfig.from_environment()
    )

    auth_engine = create_async_database_engine()

    auth_session_factory = (
        create_async_session_factory(
            auth_engine,
        )
    )

    auth_repository = (
        PostgreSQLAuthenticationRepository(
            auth_session_factory,
        )
    )

    email_provider_manager = EmailProviderManager()

    authentication_email_sender = AuthenticationEmailSenderAdapter(
        provider_manager=email_provider_manager,
    )

    authentication_email_service = AuthenticationEmailService(
        authentication_email_sender,
        frontend_base_url=_required_environment(
            "EASYCHATBOT_PORTAL_URL"
        ),
    )

    authentication_service = AuthenticationService(
        auth_repository,
        email_service=authentication_email_service,
    )

    # Provider-usage repository is synchronous by
    # contract. It therefore receives a synchronous
    # engine/session factory.
    #
    # It must not receive the async authentication
    # session factory.
    provider_usage_engine = (
        create_database_engine()
    )

    persistence_diagnostics_service = PersistenceDiagnosticsService(
        metadata=Base.metadata,
        project_root=Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1],
        engine=provider_usage_engine,
    )

    provider_usage_session_factory = (
        create_session_factory(
            provider_usage_engine,
        )
    )

    provider_usage_repository = (
        PostgreSQLProviderUsageRepository(
            provider_usage_session_factory,
        )
    )

    provider_usage_dashboard_service = (
        ProviderUsageDashboardService(
            repository=provider_usage_repository,
        )
    )

    functionality_control_repository = (
        PostgreSQLFunctionalityControlRepository(
            provider_usage_session_factory,
        )
    )

    functionality_control_authorizer = (
        AuthorizationFunctionalityControlAuthorizer()
    )

    functionality_control_service = (
        FunctionalityOperationalControlService(
            authorizer=functionality_control_authorizer,
            repository=functionality_control_repository,
        )
    )

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Project Control / operational health composition
    #
    # Project Control is read-only. It consumes application-owned health
    # and operational services and owns only its own PCD registry and
    # snapshot repository.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    production_health_configuration = (
        ProductionHealthConfiguration(
            database_engine=provider_usage_engine,
            telegram_bot_factory=None,
            llm_provider=None,
            llm_request_get=None,
        )
    )

    production_health_service = (
        ProductionHealthService(
            configuration=production_health_configuration,
        )
    )

    production_readiness_service = (
        production_health_service._readiness_service
    )

    operational_health_snapshot_service = (
        OperationalHealthSnapshotService(
            readiness_service=production_readiness_service,
        )
    )

    operational_event_service = (
        OperationalEventService()
    )

    operational_dashboard_service = (
        OperationalDashboardService(
            health_snapshot_service=operational_health_snapshot_service,
            event_service=operational_event_service,
        )
    )

    privacy_governance_composition = (
        get_privacy_governance_composition()
    )

    project_registry = (
        InMemoryProjectRegistry()
    )

    project_snapshot_repository = (
        InMemorySnapshotRepository()
    )

    project_control_service = (
        create_project_control_service(
            registry=project_registry,
            snapshots=project_snapshot_repository,
            health_service=production_health_service,
            dashboard_service=operational_dashboard_service,
            case_registry=(
                privacy_governance_composition.case_registry
            ),
        )
    )

    academy_one_week_workflow = None
    announcement_telegram_client = None

    if academy_config.activation_allowed:
        academy_session_factory = (
            auth_session_factory
        )

        (
            announcement_telegram_client,
            _announcement_publisher,
        ) = _create_announcement_components()

        academy_one_week_workflow = (
            create_academy_one_week_workflow(
                session_factory=academy_session_factory,
                telegram_bot=announcement_telegram_client,
            )
        )

    scheduler_runtime = (
        create_cybersecurity_scheduler_runtime(
            academy_one_week_workflow=academy_one_week_workflow,
        )
    )

    application = FastAPI(
        title="EasyChatBot API",
        description="AI character learning platform API",
        version="0.1.0",
        lifespan=lifespan,
    )

    application.state.scheduler_runtime = (
        scheduler_runtime
    )

    application.state.auth_repository = (
        auth_repository
    )

    application.state.authentication_service = (
        authentication_service
    )

    application.state.auth_engine = (
        auth_engine
    )

    application.state.provider_usage_engine = (
        provider_usage_engine
    )

    application.state.provider_usage_dashboard_service = (
        provider_usage_dashboard_service
    )

    application.state.functionality_control_service = (
        functionality_control_service
    )

    application.state.project_control_service = (
        project_control_service
    )

    application.state.production_health_service = (
        production_health_service
    )

    application.state.operational_dashboard_service = (
        operational_dashboard_service
    )

    application.state.announcement_telegram_client = (
        announcement_telegram_client
    )

    application.add_middleware(
        CORSMiddleware,
        allow_origins=[
            "http://localhost:3000",
            "http://127.0.0.1:3000",
            "http://localhost:5173",
            "http://127.0.0.1:5173",
        ],
        allow_credentials=True,
        allow_methods=["*"],
        allow_headers=["*"],
    )

    application.include_router(
        authentication_router,
    )

    application.include_router(
        voice_calls_router,
    )

    application.include_router(
        voice_reconnect_router,
    )

    application.include_router(
        voice_call_trigger_router,
    )

    application.include_router(
        voice_notification_acceptance_router,
    )

    operations_center_composition = (
        create_operations_center_composition(
            provider_usage_dashboard_service=(
                provider_usage_dashboard_service
            ),
            functionality_control_service=(
                functionality_control_service
            ),
        )
    )

    application.include_router(
        create_operations_center_router(
            composition=operations_center_composition,
        ),
    )

    application.include_router(
        create_persistence_operations_router(
            persistence_diagnostics_service,
            operator_dependency=require_permission(
                Permission.INTERNAL_DATA_READ,
            ),
        )
    )

    application.include_router(
        create_project_control_router(
            project_control=project_control_service,
        ),
    )

    application.include_router(
        create_email_workflows_router(),
    )

    application.include_router(
        privacy_governance_router,
    )

    processing_registry = (
        create_beta1_processing_registry()
    )

    screening_service = ScreeningService()

    screening_adapter = (
        ProcessingActivityScreeningAdapter(
            screening_service,
        )
    )

    application.include_router(
        create_privacy_screening_router(
            processing_registry=processing_registry,
            screening_adapter=screening_adapter,
        ),
    )

    application.include_router(
        provider_management_controls_router,
    )

    application.include_router(
        governance_audit_router,
    )

    application.include_router(
        governance_operations_router,
    )

    application.include_router(
        governance_evidence_router,
    )

    application.include_router(
        governance_change_lifecycle_router,
    )

    application.include_router(
        cybersecurity_intelligence_router,
    )

    application.include_router(
        cybersecurity_decision_support_router,
    )

    application.include_router(
        cybersecurity_reports_router,
    )

    application.include_router(
        cybersecurity_live_reports_router,
    )

    # Academy World ? Static Questionnaires
    #
    # This exposes:
    # GET /api/academy/worlds/{world_id}/questionnaires
    #
    # Questionnaire content remains controlled/static.
    # Recommendation metadata is handled separately.
    application.include_router(
        academy_questionnaires_router,
    )

    return application


app = create_application()


__all__ = [
    "app",
    "create_application",
]

from __future__ import annotations

from app.domain.authorization.permissions import Permission, has_permission
from app.domain.identity.principal import Principal


class AuthenticationRequiredError(PermissionError):
    """No valid authenticated principal exists."""


class PermissionDeniedError(PermissionError):
    """Authenticated principal lacks a permission."""


class AuthorizationService:
    """Provider-neutral authorization boundary."""

    def require_authenticated(
        self,
        principal: Principal | None,
    ) -> Principal:
        if principal is None or not principal.is_active:
            raise AuthenticationRequiredError(
                "Authenticated application identity is required."
            )
        return principal

    def require_permission(
        self,
        *,
        principal: Principal | None,
        permission: Permission,
    ) -> Principal:
        authenticated = self.require_authenticated(principal)

        if not has_permission(
            roles=authenticated.roles,
            permission=permission,
        ):
            raise PermissionDeniedError(
                f"Permission required: {permission.value}."
            )

        return authenticated

import OperationsCenterShell from "@/app/components/operations-center/OperationsCenterShell";
import TemplateDrivenPublicationEditor from "@/app/components/operations-center/announcement-operations/TemplateDrivenPublicationEditor";

    <OperationsCenterShell>
      <TemplateDrivenPublicationEditor />

####################################################################
FILE: .\frontend\app\components\operations-center\announcement-operations\TemplateDrivenPublicationEditor.tsx
####################################################################
"use client";

import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";
import {
  createTemplateDraft,
  getEditorTemplates,
  previewTemplate,
  type TemplateEditorOption,
} from "@/app/lib/api/announcementTemplateEditor";

export default function TemplateDrivenPublicationEditor() {
  const [templates, setTemplates] = useState<TemplateEditorOption[]>([]);
  const [templateId, setTemplateId] = useState("");
  const [values, setValues] = useState<Record<string, string>>({});
  const [title, setTitle] = useState("");
  const [preview, setPreview] = useState("");
  const [loadingTemplates, setLoadingTemplates] = useState(true);
  const [previewing, setPreviewing] = useState(false);
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [message, setMessage] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const selectedTemplate = useMemo(
    () => templates.find((item) => item.template_id === templateId) ?? null,
    [templateId, templates],
  );

  useEffect(() => {
    getEditorTemplates()
      .then((items) => {
        setTemplates(items);
        if (items.length > 0) {
          setTemplateId(items[0].template_id);
        }
      })
      .catch((reason: unknown) => {
        setError(
          reason instanceof Error
            ? reason.message
            : "Announcement types could not be loaded.",
        );
      })
      .finally(() => setLoadingTemplates(false));
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!selectedTemplate) {
      setValues({});
      setPreview("");
      return;
    }

    const nextValues: Record<string, string> = {};
    for (const field of selectedTemplate.fields) {
      nextValues[field.name] = "";
    }

    setValues(nextValues);
    setPreview("");
    setError(null);
  }, [selectedTemplate]);

  const requiredFieldsMissing = selectedTemplate
    ? selectedTemplate.fields.some(
        (field) => field.required && !values[field.name]?.trim(),
      )
    : true;

  const canPreview =
    Boolean(selectedTemplate) &&
    Boolean(title.trim()) &&
    !requiredFieldsMissing;

  function updateValue(name: string, value: string) {
    setValues((current) => ({ ...current, [name]: value }));
    setMessage(null);
  }

  async function handlePreview() {
    if (!selectedTemplate || !canPreview) {
      return;
    }

    setPreviewing(true);
    setError(null);
    setMessage(null);

    try {
      const result = await previewTemplate({
        templateId: selectedTemplate.template_id,
        templateVersion: selectedTemplate.version,
        values,
      });
      setPreview(result.content);
    } catch (reason: unknown) {
      setError(
        reason instanceof Error
          ? reason.message
          : "Preview could not be generated.",
      );
    } finally {
      setPreviewing(false);
    }
  }

  async function handleSaveDraft() {
    if (!selectedTemplate || !canPreview) {
      return;
    }

    setSaving(true);
    setError(null);
    setMessage(null);

    try {
      const result = await createTemplateDraft({
        templateId: selectedTemplate.template_id,
        templateVersion: selectedTemplate.version,
        title: title.trim(),
        values,
      });

      setMessage(
        `Draft ${result.id} saved from ${result.template_id} v${result.template_version}.`,
      );
    } catch (reason: unknown) {
      setError(
        reason instanceof Error
          ? reason.message
          : "The draft could not be saved.",
      );
    } finally {
      setSaving(false);
    }
  }

  return (
    <main className="min-h-full p-6 lg:p-10">
      <div className="mb-8">
        <p className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-fuchsia-300">
          Announcement Operations
        </p>
        <h1 className="mt-2 text-3xl font-bold text-white">
          Create publication
        </h1>
        <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-slate-400">
          Select an approved announcement type, complete its controlled fields,
          preview it, and save a draft.
        </p>
      </div>

      {error && (
        <div
          role="alert"
          className="mb-6 rounded-2xl border border-rose-500/30 bg-rose-500/10 p-4 text-sm text-rose-200"
        >
          {error}
        </div>
      )}

      {message && (
        <div className="mb-6 rounded-2xl border border-emerald-500/30 bg-emerald-500/10 p-4 text-sm text-emerald-200">
          {message}
        </div>
      )}

      <div className="grid gap-6 xl:grid-cols-2">
        <section className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-800 bg-slate-950/70 p-6">
          <h2 className="font-semibold text-white">Publication content</h2>

          <label
            htmlFor="announcement-type"
            className="mt-5 block text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-500"
          >
            Announcement type
          </label>

          <select
            id="announcement-type"
            aria-label="Announcement type"
            value={templateId}
            disabled={loadingTemplates || templates.length === 0}
            onChange={(event) => setTemplateId(event.target.value)}
            className="mt-2 w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-700 bg-slate-900 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-white outline-none focus:border-fuchsia-500 disabled:opacity-50"
          >
            {loadingTemplates && (
              <option value="">Loading announcement types…</option>
            )}
            {!loadingTemplates && templates.length === 0 && (
              <option value="">No announcement types available</option>
            )}
            {templates.map((template) => (
              <option key={template.template_id} value={template.template_id}>
                {template.name}
              </option>
            ))}
          </select>

          {selectedTemplate && (
            <div className="mt-3 rounded-xl border border-slate-800 bg-slate-900/60 p-4">
              <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
                <span className="rounded-full bg-fuchsia-500/10 px-2.5 py-1 text-xs font-semibold text-fuchsia-300">
                  {selectedTemplate.category}
                </span>
                <span className="text-xs text-slate-500">
                  Approved version {selectedTemplate.version}
                </span>
              </div>
              <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-slate-400">
                {selectedTemplate.description}
              </p>
            </div>
          )}

          <label
            htmlFor="announcement-title"
            className="mt-5 block text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-500"
          >
            Publication title
          </label>
          <input
            id="announcement-title"
            value={title}
            onChange={(event) => setTitle(event.target.value)}
            className="mt-2 w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-700 bg-slate-900 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-white outline-none focus:border-fuchsia-500"
            placeholder="Announcement title"
          />

          {selectedTemplate?.fields.map((field) => (
            <div key={field.name}>
              <label
                htmlFor={`template-field-${field.name}`}
                className="mt-5 block text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-500"
              >
                {field.label}
                {field.required && (
                  <span className="ml-1 text-fuchsia-300">*</span>
                )}
              </label>

              {field.multiline ? (
                <textarea
                  id={`template-field-${field.name}`}
                  value={values[field.name] ?? ""}
                  onChange={(event) =>
                    updateValue(field.name, event.target.value)
                  }
                  rows={5}
                  className="mt-2 w-full resize-y rounded-xl border border-slate-700 bg-slate-900 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-white outline-none focus:border-fuchsia-500"
                />
              ) : (
                <input
                  id={`template-field-${field.name}`}
                  value={values[field.name] ?? ""}
                  onChange={(event) =>
                    updateValue(field.name, event.target.value)
                  }
                  className="mt-2 w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-700 bg-slate-900 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-white outline-none focus:border-fuchsia-500"
                />
              )}
            </div>
          ))}

          {selectedTemplate && requiredFieldsMissing && (
            <p className="mt-4 text-xs text-amber-300">
              Complete all required fields before previewing or saving.
            </p>
          )}

          <div className="mt-5 flex flex-wrap gap-3">
            <button
              type="button"
              disabled={!canPreview || previewing}
              onClick={handlePreview}
              className="rounded-xl border border-fuchsia-500/50 px-5 py-3 text-sm font-semibold text-fuchsia-200 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-40"
            >
              {previewing ? "Previewing…" : "Preview"}
            </button>

            <button
              type="button"
              disabled={!canPreview || saving}
              onClick={handleSaveDraft}
              className="rounded-xl bg-fuchsia-500 px-5 py-3 text-sm font-semibold text-white disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-40"
            >
              {saving ? "Saving…" : "Save draft"}
            </button>
          </div>
        </section>

        <section className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-800 bg-slate-950/70 p-6">
          <p className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-fuchsia-300">
            Preview
          </p>

          <div className="mt-4 rounded-2xl border border-slate-800 bg-[#050816] p-6">
            <p className="text-xs uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-500">
              EasyChatBot Academy
            </p>
            <h2 className="mt-3 text-2xl font-bold text-white">
              {title || "Announcement title"}
            </h2>
            <p className="mt-4 whitespace-pre-wrap text-sm leading-7 text-slate-300">
              {preview ||
                "Complete the selected announcement type and choose Preview to see the rendered announcement."}
            </p>
          </div>
        </section>
      </div>
    </main>
  );
}

####################################################################
FILE: .\frontend\app\lib\api\announcementTemplateEditor.ts
####################################################################
export interface TemplateEditorField {
  name: string;
  required: boolean;
  label: string;
  multiline: boolean;
}

export interface TemplateEditorOption {
  template_id: string;
  name: string;
  category: string;
  description: string;
  version: number;
  publication_type: string;
  fields: TemplateEditorField[];
}

export interface TemplateEditorPreview {
  template_id: string;
  template_version: number;
  publication_type: string;
  content: string;
}

export interface CreatedDraftPublication {
  id: string;
  title: string;
  content: string;
  template_id: string;
  template_version: number;
  publication_type: string;
  status: string;
  created_at: string | null;
}

const API_BASE =
  process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_API_BASE_URL ?? "http://localhost:8000";

async function request<T>(
  path: string,
  init?: RequestInit,
): Promise<T> {
  const response = await fetch(`${API_BASE}${path}`, {
    ...init,
    headers: {
      Accept: "application/json",
      "Content-Type": "application/json",
      ...(init?.headers ?? {}),
    },
    cache: "no-store",
  });

  if (!response.ok) {
    let detail = `Announcement template API returned ${response.status}.`;

    try {
      const body = (await response.json()) as { detail?: string };
      if (body.detail) {
        detail = body.detail;
      }
    } catch {
      // Preserve the stable HTTP error for non-JSON responses.
    }

    throw new Error(detail);
  }

  return response.json() as Promise<T>;
}

export function getEditorTemplates(): Promise<TemplateEditorOption[]> {
  return request<TemplateEditorOption[]>(
    "/api/announcement-operations/template-editor/templates",
  );
}

export function previewTemplate(input: {
  templateId: string;
  templateVersion: number;
  values: Record<string, string>;
}): Promise<TemplateEditorPreview> {
  return request<TemplateEditorPreview>(
    "/api/announcement-operations/template-editor/preview",
    {
      method: "POST",
      body: JSON.stringify({
        template_id: input.templateId,
        template_version: input.templateVersion,
        values: input.values,
      }),
    },
  );
}

export function createTemplateDraft(input: {
  templateId: string;
  templateVersion: number;
  title: string;
  values: Record<string, string>;
}): Promise<CreatedDraftPublication> {
  return request<CreatedDraftPublication>(
    "/api/announcement-operations/template-editor/drafts",
    {
      method: "POST",
      body: JSON.stringify({
        template_id: input.templateId,
        template_version: input.templateVersion,
        title: input.title,
        values: input.values,
      }),
    },
  );
}

====================================================================
16. REQUIRED R10 QUESTIONS
====================================================================

R10 MUST ANSWER:

1. What is the single production announcement API entry point?

2. Which exact router/file owns that entry point?

3. Is the router registered in the authoritative production
   application?

4. What exact authentication dependency provides the current principal?

5. What exact AuthorizationService dependency enforces
   Permission.ANNOUNCEMENT_PUBLISH?

6. Which announcement mutations require ANNOUNCEMENT_PUBLISH?

7. Is actor identity derived exclusively from the authenticated
   principal?

8. Can a caller submit an arbitrary actor in the request body?

9. What is the canonical Publication lifecycle used by production?

10. Which existing application services can be reused unchanged?

11. Which exact files are permitted to change?

12. Which existing files MUST remain untouched?

13. How is the legacy AdminAuthorizer path isolated, migrated,
    deprecated, or removed without creating a bypass?

14. Can any API/UI path directly invoke Telegram?

15. Can any API/UI path directly invoke publisher.publish() without
    the approved/durable execution boundary?

16. Does Beta access fail closed?

17. What exact unit/component/integration/E2E/regression tests must
    pass before implementation is accepted?

18. What exact smoke test demonstrates the complete production path?

19. Are there pre-existing dirty-worktree changes that make any
    proposed file unsafe to modify without reconciliation?

20. What are Reviewer 1 and Reviewer 2's independent verdicts?

====================================================================
17. REQUIRED IMPLEMENTATION FILE ALLOWLIST
====================================================================

R10 MUST NOT merely say "backend changes".

It must identify exact permitted files, for example:

- router/API file
- composition file
- main.py
- frontend API/editor file(s)
- tests

Every file outside the approved allowlist is considered untouched.

====================================================================
18. REQUIRED 4-EYES DECISION
====================================================================

Reviewer 1:
VERDICT: PASS / FAIL / BLOCKED
NAME/ROLE:
DATE:
COMMENTS:

Reviewer 2:
VERDICT: PASS / FAIL / BLOCKED
NAME/ROLE:
DATE:
COMMENTS:

FINAL R10 VERDICT:
PASS / FAIL / BLOCKED

IMPLEMENTATION AUTHORIZATION:
AUTHORIZED / NOT AUTHORIZED

====================================================================
19. STOP CONDITIONS
====================================================================

Implementation MUST remain blocked if:

- production entry point is ambiguous
- more than one authoritative mutation path exists
- authenticated principal cannot be established
- ANNOUNCEMENT_PUBLISH cannot be enforced
- actor remains caller-controlled
- legacy AdminAuthorizer remains an exploitable bypass
- approval can be bypassed
- publication can bypass durable/approved execution
- direct Telegram publication exists outside the controlled boundary
- Beta denial is not testable
- exact implementation file allowlist cannot be established
- dirty worktree creates unresolved ambiguity
- required tests cannot be defined
- either 4-eyes reviewer returns FAIL or BLOCKED
- final R10 authorization is not explicitly AUTHORIZED

====================================================================
END OF R10 INSPECTION
